# GB(4,4) complete classification — n = 20–21

Complete classification of the generalized bicycle codes GB(4,4) of length `n` = 5 to 25: for each parameter `[[N, k, d]]` (`N = 2n`), every pair `(A, B)` of polynomials found by the search (see `../algorithms/gb44_all_representatives.m`).

Each length `n` lists its best parameters: for each dimension `k`, the parameters of greatest distance `d`.

<details>
<summary><b>n = 20</b> — best parameters: [[40, 2, 8]], [[40, 4, 8]], [[40, 6, 6]], [[40, 8, 5]], [[40, 10, 4]], [[40, 12, 4]], [[40, 14, 4]], [[40, 16, 3]], [[40, 22, 2]], [[40, 24, 2]], [[40, 30, 2]]</summary>
